# Source-weight rescan (t53u)

Read-only. No grade, lock, or catalog row was changed. The Sprouts section was not edited.

## Rule

Token recommendation rule, `docs/PROJECT_NOTES.md` line 2203 (PR #1000):

> When sources disagree on a token, the integrative sources hold more weight. The integrative sources are NCCIH, MedlinePlus, Office of Dietary Supplements (ODS), NatMed Pro, Memorial Sloan Kettering About Herbs, PubMed/CAM, Andrew Weil, Mayo Clinic, and LiverTox. A majority of those sources decides the grade. If that majority contradicts FDA, CIR, or FEMA, the integrative majority still rules.

A source votes only when its page states a safety position that maps to one grade. A shared word is not coverage: the ODS magnesium sheet does not grade magnesium stearate, and a ginger page does not grade Quik-Sorb. Efficacy-only sentences do not vote. "Not enough evidence that it works" is not a grade.

Mapping used, from Methodology §4a and the condition rule: likely safe or well tolerated in ordinary use, with no serious harm mechanism, maps to Cleared. Documented harm that depends on dose, person, or route, including a food-safe call the app cannot check, maps to Caution. A page that says do not use, or that names fatal or established toxic injury for that form, maps to Avoid. A page that states both a clear safe call and a clear harm call, without one of them being the condition rule, does not vote. Limited is not used unless the page describes a vague mixture or an emerging signal of that kind. None of the pages read did that.

## What was opened

- NCCIH Herbs at a Glance, https://www.nccih.nih.gov/health/herbsataglance , and the A–Z list, https://www.nccih.nih.gov/health/atoz . Safety sections were read for the herb pages cited below.
- MedlinePlus herbs and supplements, https://medlineplus.gov/druginfo/herb_All.html . The page says the Therapeutic Research Center Natural Medicines content has been unavailable since July 29, 2025. No independent MedlinePlus grade was available. Position: not covered.
- ODS fact-sheet list, https://ods.od.nih.gov/factsheets/list-all/ . ODS-authored consumer or health-professional sheets are vitamins, minerals, and a short list that includes black cohosh. Those sheets were not opened as full pages in this pass, so ODS does not vote below. A mineral name inside a different chemical is not an ODS position.
- NatMed Pro, https://naturalmedicines.therapeuticresearch.com/ . The fetch returned HTTP 403. NCCIH cites it as a subscription database. Position: paywalled, not accessible.
- MSK About Herbs. The herb sitemap lists 308 pages (https://www.mskcc.org/sitemap.xml?entity_type=node&type=herb). Adverse-reaction text was read for the pages cited below.
- PubMed/CAM. NCCIH describes PubMed as a citation index, not a grade: https://www.nccih.nih.gov/health/how-to-find-information-about-complementary-health-approaches-on-pubmed . No paper was synthesized into a position. Position: not covered.
- Andrew Weil. The first herb index page was opened: https://www.drweil.com/vitamins-supplements-herbs/herbs/ . Later index pages returned HTTP 403, so individual Weil articles were not opened. Position for ingredients not on that first page: not covered on the page that opened.
- Mayo Clinic drugs and supplements index, https://www.mayoclinic.org/drugs-supplements . The index was read. The individual articles were not opened, so Mayo does not vote.
- LiverTox herbal index, https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/books/NBK548441/ , and the agent index, https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/books/NBK547852/ . Chapter pages that were requested returned a browser check and no likelihood score. LiverTox does not vote.

FDA, CIR, and FEMA were not used.

## Population

1386 locked strings in 1271 ingredient groups, after spelling variants were grouped and prose that is not a token was left out. The strings come from the methodology table and changelog and from the exact sets and equality checks in `lib/scan-preview/plainLanguage.ts` and `lib/scan-preview/limitedStackEligibility.ts`. Where those sources disagreed, the grade the scanner checks first was kept.

Groups with more than one current grade list each string with its own grade. Named natural-flavor strings are one group. Quik-Sorb prints are one group. Potassium sorbate prints, including the misspelling, are one group. Fatty Acids (Stabilizer) and Fatty Acids (Coating Agent) are one group. Gummy, topical, and fill forms stay in their own groups.

| no-change (two or more sources voted, and the majority matches the current grade) | 8 |
| insufficient-coverage (fewer than two sources voted) | 1252 |
| no-majority (two or more sources voted and they do not agree) | 10 |
| would change | 1 |

## Tokens that would change

### glycyrrhiza extract licorice extract oral inactive

- `Glycyrrhiza extract / licorice extract (oral inactive)` — current Cleared, proposed Caution.

NCCIH says licorice root is generally safe as a food ingredient, and that glycyrrhizin can cause irregular heartbeat and cardiac arrest in large amounts or with long use, including severe effects from small amounts in people who eat a lot of salt or who have hypertension, heart disease, or kidney disease (https://www.nccih.nih.gov/health/licorice-root). MSK lists hypertension, hypokalemia, and cardiac arrhythmias among adverse reactions (https://www.mskcc.org/cancer-care/integrative-medicine/herbs/licorice). Both map to Caution: the harm is real and the amount and the person are conditions this app cannot check, so the food-safe call drops one level. Current lock is Cleared.

- soy (bare / unspecified) (Limited)
- soy peptones (Limited)
- Stevia extract (Caution)

- Stevia Extract (Stevioside) (Caution)
- stevia (leaf) extract (Caution); stevia leaf extract (Caution)
- Stevia; rebaudioside a (extracted and purified from natural stevia leaf); Stevia extract; stevia leaf extract (Cleared)
- Stevia / steviol glycosides, high-purity extract (Cleared)
- Whole-leaf / crude stevia (Caution)

Every other group is in the table below. For those, a single unread Weil or LiverTox page would still leave fewer than two votes, so the result would stay no change.
